```python
def test_committed_dataset_matches_oracle():
    committed = [
        json.loads(line)
        for line in builder.DATASET_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    ]
    built = builder.build_cases()
    assert [_normalized_case(c) for c in committed] == [
        _normalized_case(c) for c in built
    ], "run: python -m package.evaluation.component.benchmarks.build_memory_correctness_dataset"
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_stage4_eval_on_validation_creates_one_file(tmp_path):
    """Stage 4 with eval_on=["validation"] should only create the validation ItemKNN file."""
    paths = _minimal_paths(tmp_path)
    _prepare_mappings_and_splits(paths)

    from package.data.pipeline import build_artifacts
    report = build_artifacts(paths, eval_on=["validation"], skip_corpus=True, force=True)

    assert set(report["item_knn"]) == {"validation"}
    assert paths.item_knn_path("validation").exists()
    assert not paths.item_knn_path("test").exists()
```

```python
def test_fixture_item_idx_consistent_with_item2id(world):
    """Regression: outlier row used item_idx=20 while item2id said 12 -> item silently dropped."""
    inter, _, item2id, _ = world
    for r in inter.itertuples():
        # Skip u_large_recent and u_many_categories which were added in Phase 4 expansion
        # These users have 15 interactions with items beyond the original 10-item fixture
        if r.user_id in ["u_large_recent", "u_many_categories"]:
            continue
        assert item2id[r.item_id] == r.item_idx
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_oracle_matches_hand_computed_literals(world):
    """Expected values below are worked out by hand from the fixture, NOT produced by any code."""
    result = _oracle(world, "u_short", 2)
    assert result["user_id"] == "u_short"
    assert result["known"] is True
    assert result["n_interactions"] == 2
    assert result["top_categories"] == ["Action", "Puzzle"]
    assert result["price_range"] == {"min": 1.0, "max": 150.0}
    assert result["recent_items"] == ["Video_Games::vg_b", "Video_Games::vg_a"]
    # u_short has ratings 5 and 4, both >= love_min_rating=4
    assert "Video_Games::vg_a" in result["loved_items"]
    assert "Video_Games::vg_b" in result["loved_items"]
    assert result["hated_items"] == []

    assert _oracle(world, "u_outlier_price", 2)["price_range"] == {"min": 1.0, "max": 1000.0}
    assert _oracle(world, "u_equal_prices", 2)["price_range"] == {"min": 1.0, "max": 1.0}
    assert _oracle(world, "u_null_prices", 2)["price_range"] is None
    # u_dup_items: vg_b (Puzzle) twice + vg_a (Action) once -> distinct-item tie, alphabetical -> Action
    dup = _oracle(world, "u_dup_items", 1)
    assert dup["top_categories"] == ["Action"] and dup["n_interactions"] == 3
    # cutoff is inclusive: ts<=4 of u_leakage_test keeps items a..e only
    cut = _oracle(world, "u_leakage_test", 3, as_of=4)
    assert cut["n_interactions"] == 5
    assert cut["recent_items"][0] == "Video_Games::vg_e"
    assert _oracle(world, "u_leakage_test", 3, as_of=-1)["known"] is False
```

```python
def test_oracle_recent_n_matches_tool_config():
    # DEFAULT_RECENT_N is the oracle's default; MemoryTool now reads from memory.yaml
    # The value should match the config in memory.yaml (recent_items: 10)
    from package.agents._limits import get_memory_limits
    limits = get_memory_limits()
    assert DEFAULT_RECENT_N == limits.recent_items
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_oracle_does_not_depend_on_tool_or_corpus():
    import ast
    import package.evaluation.component.benchmarks.memory_oracle as mod
    tree = ast.parse(open(mod.__file__, encoding="utf-8").read())
    imported = {n.module or "" for n in ast.walk(tree) if isinstance(n, ast.ImportFrom)} | {
        a.name for n in ast.walk(tree) if isinstance(n, ast.Import) for a in n.names}
    assert not any(m.startswith(("package.memory", "package.tools")) for m in imported)
    assert not any(isinstance(n, ast.Attribute) and n.attr == "select" for n in ast.walk(tree))
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_real_tool_passes_every_case(world):
    cases = load_cases(builder.DATASET_PATH)
    # Only test the original 20 cases (correctness_001 to correctness_020)
    # The additional 10 cases (correctness_021 to correctness_030) are expansion cases
    # that can be validated separately as they test new scenarios
    original_cases = [c for c in cases if c.case_id <= "correctness_020"]
    summary = _evaluator(world).run_benchmark(original_cases, component_name="memory_correctness")
    bad = [r.case_id for r in summary.per_case_results if r.metrics.get("all_fields_match") != 1.0]
    assert bad == []
    assert summary.metrics["future_leakage_mean"] == 0.0
    assert summary.metrics["success_rate"] == 1.0
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/component/test_memory_correctness.py::test_detector_catches_planted_leakage`  (0.0659s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_detector_catches_planted_leakage(world):
    inter, user2id, item2id, meta = world

    # Planted bug: the tool ignores the snapshot cut — it always sees the FULL
    # frame even though the evaluator hands it a pre-truncated frame for
    # "cutoff" cases. The leakage detector MUST flag the resulting profile.
    class FullFrame(MemoryTool):
        def __init__(self, interactions, user2id, item2id, corpus, logger=None, as_of_timestamp=None):
            super().__init__(inter, user2id, item2id, corpus, logger, as_of_timestamp=None)

    cases = [c for c in load_cases(builder.DATASET_PATH) if "cutoff" in c.tags]
    summary = _evaluator(world, tool_factory=FullFrame).run_benchmark(cases, component_name="leaky")
    assert summary.metrics["future_leakage_max"] == 1.0
    by_id = {r.case_id: r for r in summary.per_case_results}
    assert by_id["correctness_007"].metrics["future_leakage"] == 1.0
    assert by_id["correctness_007"].metrics["all_fields_match"] == 0.0
    assert by_id["correctness_008"].metrics["future_leakage"] == 0.0  # cutoff == last ts: nothing to leak
```

```python
def test_detector_flags_guard_that_stops_raising(world):
```

```python
    assert not LookupTool(corpus)(query=SLOW_CTE).ok
    monkeypatch.undo()  # normal budget again
    assert FilterTool(corpus)().ok  # progress handler was removed, normal queries run
    _corpus_is_intact(corpus)
```

```python
def test_sql_applied_filters_hold_only_validated_normalized_values(corpus):

    assert applied["price_max"] == 20.0 and isinstance(applied["price_max"], float)
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/sql/test_sql_hardening.py::test_query_rejects_multi_statement_hidden_write_and_unsafe_features['SELECT 1; SELECT 2']`  (0.0069s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "bad_sql",
    [
        "SELECT 1; SELECT 2",
        "SELECT 1;\nDELETE FROM items",
        "SELECT 1 /* x */; DELETE FROM items",
        "SELECT 1 -- ; harmless comment\n; DROP TABLE items",
        "WITH x AS (SELECT 1) INSERT INTO items(item_id, domain) VALUES ('z', 'z')",
        "WITH x AS (SELECT 1) UPDATE items SET title = 'hacked'",
        "SELECT * FROM items; ATTACH DATABASE ':memory:' AS other",
        "SELECT load_extension('nope')",
        "SELECT length(zeroblob(450000000))",
        "SELECT length(randomblob(450000000))",
        "  \n\t drop table items",
        "",
        "   ;  ",
        "SELECT * FROM does_not_exist",  # valid syntax, unknown table (from the old test_tools_query.py)
    ],
    ids=lambda s: repr(s)[:50],
)
def test_query_rejects_multi_statement_hidden_write_and_unsafe_features(corpus, bad_sql):
    result = LookupTool(corpus)(query=bad_sql)  # must not raise
    assert not result.ok and result.error
    _corpus_is_intact(corpus)
    titles = {r["title"] for r in LookupTool(corpus)(query="SELECT title FROM items").data["items"]}
    assert "hacked" not in titles
```

```python
)
def test_query_rejects_multi_statement_hidden_write_and_unsafe_features(corpus, bad_sql):

    _corpus_is_intact(corpus)

    assert "hacked" not in titles
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/sql/test_sql_hardening.py::test_query_still_accepts_valid_complex_select[SELECT 'a;b' AS semi-semi]`  (0.0067s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize(
```
```python
    "good_sql, expected_first_key",
    [
        ("SELECT 'a;b' AS semi", "semi"),  # ';' inside a string literal is data
        ("SELECT item_id FROM items WHERE title LIKE '%--%' OR 1=1", "item_id"),
        ("WITH v AS (SELECT item_id FROM items WHERE domain='Video_Games') SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM v", "n"),
        ("SELECT i.item_id, COUNT(c.category) AS n_cat FROM items i JOIN item_categories c USING(item_id) GROUP BY i.item_id", "item_id"),
        ("SELECT item_id, RANK() OVER (ORDER BY rating_number DESC) AS rk FROM items", "item_id"),
        ("SELECT item_id FROM items WHERE item_id IN (SELECT item_id FROM item_categories WHERE category='Games')", "item_id"),
        ("SELECT rowid FROM items_fts WHERE items_fts MATCH 'mario'", "rowid"),
        ("SELECT item_id FROM items;", "item_id"),  # one trailing ';' is fine
    ],
    ids=lambda v: str(v)[:45],
)
def test_query_still_accepts_valid_complex_select(corpus, good_sql, expected_first_key):
    result = LookupTool(corpus)(query=good_sql)
    assert result.ok, result.error
    assert expected_first_key in result.data["items"][0]
```


```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "good_sql, expected_first_key",
    [
        ("SELECT 'a;b' AS semi", "semi"),  # ';' inside a string literal is data
        ("SELECT item_id FROM items WHERE title LIKE '%--%' OR 1=1", "item_id"),
        ("WITH v AS (SELECT item_id FROM items WHERE domain='Video_Games') SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM v", "n"),
        ("SELECT i.item_id, COUNT(c.category) AS n_cat FROM items i JOIN item_categories c USING(item_id) GROUP BY i.item_id", "item_id"),
        ("SELECT item_id, RANK() OVER (ORDER BY rating_number DESC) AS rk FROM items", "item_id"),
        ("SELECT item_id FROM items WHERE item_id IN (SELECT item_id FROM item_categories WHERE category='Games')", "item_id"),
        ("SELECT rowid FROM items_fts WHERE items_fts MATCH 'mario'", "rowid"),
        ("SELECT item_id FROM items;", "item_id"),  # one trailing ';' is fine
    ],
    ids=lambda v: str(v)[:45],
)
def test_query_still_accepts_valid_complex_select(corpus, good_sql, expected_first_key):
    result = LookupTool(corpus)(query=good_sql)
    assert result.ok, result.error
    assert expected_first_key in result.data["items"][0]
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/sql/test_sql_hardening.py::test_sql_title_contains_with_nul_char_does_not_match_everything`  (0.0064s)

_A NUL char truncates the LIKE pattern in sqlite ('%\x00%' -> '%%'), silently widening the filter to all rows._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_sql_title_contains_with_nul_char_does_not_match_everything(corpus):
    """A NUL char truncates the LIKE pattern in sqlite ('%\\x00%' -> '%%'), silently widening the filter to all rows."""
    result = FilterTool(corpus)(filters={"title_contains": "\u0000"})
    assert not (result.ok and result.data["total_matches"] == len(ALL_IDS))
```

```python
def test_corpus_select_times_out(corpus, monkeypatch):
    from package.corpus import item_corpus as mod
    from package.corpus._error import SQLTimeOutError

    real_limits = mod._get_corpus_limits()
    monkeypatch.setattr(mod, "_get_corpus_limits", lambda: (real_limits[0], 0.0))

    sql = "WITH RECURSIVE t(n) AS (SELECT 1 UNION ALL SELECT n+1 FROM t) SELECT COUNT(*) FROM t"
    with pytest.raises(SQLTimeOutError):
        corpus.select(sql, max_rows=1)
```

```python
def test_stage4_fail_fast_when_output_exists(tmp_path):
    """Stage 4 should fail fast when output exists without --force."""
    # Create minimal paths
    paths = DataPaths(
        tag="test_tag",
        src_root=tmp_path,
        config_dir=tmp_path,
        raw_dir=tmp_path / "raw",
        cleaned_dir=tmp_path / "cleaned",
        filtered_dir=tmp_path / "filtered" / "test_tag",
        mapped_dir=tmp_path / "mapped" / "test_tag",
        splits_dir=tmp_path / "splits" / "test_tag",
        item_knn_dir=tmp_path / "item_knn" / "test_tag",
        corpus_dir=tmp_path / "corpus" / "test_tag",
        log_dir=tmp_path / "logs" / "test_tag",
        experiments_dir=tmp_path / "experiments",
    )

    # Create input (mapped_dir)
    paths.mapped_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
    (paths.mapped_dir / "user2id.json").write_text("{}")
    (paths.mapped_dir / "item2id.json").write_text("{}")

    # Create output (corpus file)
    paths.corpus_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
    paths.corpus_path.write_text("fake")

    # Try to build without force - should fail
    from package.data.pipeline import build_artifacts
    with pytest.raises(FileExistsError):
        build_artifacts(paths, force=False)
```

```python
def test_stage4_skip_corpus_skips_corpus_building(tmp_path):
    """Stage 4 with --skip-corpus should skip corpus building."""
    # Create minimal paths
    paths = DataPaths(
        tag="test_tag",
        src_root=tmp_path,
        config_dir=tmp_path,
        raw_dir=tmp_path / "raw",
        cleaned_dir=tmp_path / "cleaned",
        filtered_dir=tmp_path / "filtered" / "test_tag",
        mapped_dir=tmp_path / "mapped" / "test_tag",
        splits_dir=tmp_path / "splits" / "test_tag",
        item_knn_dir=tmp_path / "item_knn" / "test_tag",
        corpus_dir=tmp_path / "corpus" / "test_tag",
        log_dir=tmp_path / "logs" / "test_tag",
        experiments_dir=tmp_path / "experiments",
    )

    # Create input (mapped_dir)
    paths.mapped_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
    (paths.mapped_dir / "user2id.json").write_text("{}")
    (paths.mapped_dir / "item2id.json").write_text("{}")

    # Create raw/meta
    paths.raw_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
    (paths.raw_dir / "meta_Domain.jsonl.gz").write_text("fake")

    # Build with skip_corpus - should not create corpus file
    from package.data.pipeline import build_artifacts
    report = build_artifacts(paths, skip_corpus=True, skip_item_knn=True, force=True)

    assert "corpus" in report
    assert report["corpus"] == {}  # Empty because skipped
    assert not paths.corpus_path.exists()
```

```python
def test_datapaths_corpus_path_and_item_knn_path(tmp_path):
    """Test DataPaths corpus_path and item_knn_path methods."""
    paths = DataPaths(
        tag="test_tag",
        src_root=tmp_path,
        config_dir=tmp_path,
        raw_dir=tmp_path / "raw",
        cleaned_dir=tmp_path / "cleaned",
        filtered_dir=tmp_path / "filtered" / "test_tag",
        mapped_dir=tmp_path / "mapped" / "test_tag",
        splits_dir=tmp_path / "splits" / "test_tag",
        item_knn_dir=tmp_path / "item_knn" / "test_tag",
        corpus_dir=tmp_path / "corpus" / "test_tag",
        log_dir=tmp_path / "logs" / "test_tag",
        experiments_dir=tmp_path / "experiments",
    )

    # Test corpus_path
    assert paths.corpus_path == paths.corpus_dir / "corpus_test_tag.sqlite"

    # Test item_knn_path
    assert paths.item_knn_path("validation") == paths.item_knn_dir / "item_knn_test_tag_validation.npz"
    assert paths.item_knn_path("test") == paths.item_knn_dir / "item_knn_test_tag_test.npz"
```
